- original:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Ashurbanipal, leader of Assyria (Player 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Assyria
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Building: Royal Library, Replacing School of Philosophy (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Lamassu Gate, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Civilization Ability: When you conquer a City, gain a Technology already known by the owner, or if impossible, 20 Science, scaling with Era and the City's Population. All Great Works produce +3 Science.
      - Leader: Ashurbanipal
      - PlayerID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Stealth: Allows you to build the Stealth Bomber, the most powerful Ranged Unit in the game.
        Completing it would unlock: Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      
      ## Policies
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: user
      - content: # Strategies
        Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        
        ## Persona
        - VictoryCompetitiveness: 5
        - WonderCompetitiveness: 5
        - MinorCivCompetitiveness: 5
        - Boldness: 2
        - WarBias: 1
        - HostileBias: 2
        - WarmongerHate: 3
        - NeutralBias: 8
        - FriendlyBias: 9
        - GuardedBias: 4
        - AfraidBias: 4
        - DiplomaticBalance: 8
        - Friendliness: 6
        - WorkWithWillingness: 8
        - WorkAgainstWillingness: 6
        - Loyalty: 8
        - MinorCivFriendlyBias: 6
        - MinorCivNeutralBias: 7
        - MinorCivHostileBias: 4
        - MinorCivWarBias: 3
        - DenounceWillingness: 3
        - Forgiveness: 6
        - Meanness: 1
        - Neediness: 6
        - Chattiness: 5
        - DeceptiveBias: 5
        - Rationale: PEACE SURVIVAL FINAL PHASE: 8 turns to diplo vote. Persona unchanged - maximum peaceful posture. Boldness 2, WarBias 1, Meanness 1 signal no aggressive intent to Rome despite their Tarsus bomber buildup. NeutralBias 8, FriendlyBias 9 ensure diplomatic channel remains open. Guarded 4, Afraid 4 acknowledge reality (25k vs 4k) without capitulation. Portugal at 76% - likely peaceful resolution in 8 turns.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: Stealth
        - Rationale: STEALTH MAINTAINED - POST-VOTE DEFENSE PREPARATION: Current research Advanced Ballistics completes soon. Stealth unlocks Stealth Bombers for ultimate defensive capability. Regardless of World Congress outcome, need advanced air units to deter Rome's massive conventional superiority (29:1 force ratio). Stealth Bombers provide standoff strike capability against Roman concentrations.
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: People's Army (Policy)
        - Rationale: PEOPLE'S ARMY MAINTAINED - EMERGENCY DEFENSE CONTINGENCY: +100% Military Academy production and 5 free Military Academies remains critical. If Portugal's diplomatic victory succeeds, these academies provide long-term professional military. If vote fails, need immediate combat boost to survive Roman onslaught. No better defensive policy available in Order ideology.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Hanoi
        - Public: 15
        - Private: 10
        - Rationale: Hanoi offers 62 Influence and 106 Faith for building Pyramids. Establishing positive relations now positions for alliance if I pursue this wonder. Faith generation would support religion/pantheon benefits. Public friendship helps distinguish from Rome tensions.
        - UpdatedTurn: 54
        
        ### Ragusa
        - Public: -30
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: Ragusa declared war on me (Turn 180) following their conflict with The Ottomans, though peace was subsequently negotiated. They are now at -20 influence and broke my pledge of protection. While currently at peace, they sided with my enemies and must be treated with hostility. -30/-50 reflects this breach of trust and positions me to respond aggressively if they join against me again.
        - UpdatedTurn: 182
        
        ### Greece
        - Public: 5
        - Private: 0
        - Rationale: GREECE FURTHER REDUCTION: Greece at 100% war weariness, losing badly to Huns (-72%)/Portugal (-66%)/Rome (-88%). Proposal to repeal Casus Belli helps warmongers. Currently irrelevant - they're collapsing. Minimal positive 5/0 prevents joining Rome/Austria while acknowledging they're effectively neutralized by three-front war.
        - UpdatedTurn: 359
        
        ### Persia
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -30
        - Rationale: PERSIA VASSAL CAPITULATION: Persia has fully capitulated to Rome and is now a Roman vassal. Darius has betrayed our vassal relationship (previously 95/95) and is actively attacking my territory near Susa and Ecbatana with Gatling Guns and Riflemen. This represents a complete reversal - former buffer is now hostile enemy from southeast. Public 0 for potential future reconciliation, Private -30 acknowledges the betrayal and current hostilities. Combined with Rome's domination, I face a three-front war.
        - UpdatedTurn: 443
        
        ### The Ottomans
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 95
        - Rationale: OTTOMANS ALLIANCE MAINTAINED: Strong Order ideology ally providing eastern flank security. Battle-tested at Linz against Austria. Their 6102 military strength and shared ideology ensures World Congress voting alignment. Critical buffer against Austria and potential Roman aggression from east. No change needed.
        - UpdatedTurn: 462
        
        ### The Huns
        - Public: 80
        - Private: 80
        - Rationale: HUNS WESTERN BUFFER MAINTAINED: 80/80 relationship continues. Huns provide western pressure on Rome through defensive pact with Portugal. Their 4130 military strength and Order ideology helps contain Rome's 26101 strength. Shared Order ensures World Congress alignment. No change needed.
        - UpdatedTurn: 462
        
        ### Malacca
        - Public: -80
        - Private: -80
        - Rationale: MALACCA EMERGENCY HOSTILITY: Malacca (Roman allied city-state with 366 influence to Rome) declared war on me Turn 463, reducing my influence from 35 to -60 and breaking my pledge. This represents active Roman aggression through proxy. -80/-80 signals this is unacceptable aggression. However, cannot attack Malacca directly as that would give Rome war justification and tank my diplo standing before World Congress vote. Must endure defensive war, secure Portugal victory, hope war ends with diplomatic resolution.
        - UpdatedTurn: 464
        
        ### Okilis
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: OKILIS RE-ALLIANCE: Okilis fluctuated wildly (717 -> 50 -> 160) but is now my ally again. Increased to 90/85 to solidify this critical eastern buffer. Okilis provides naval support and city-state alliance benefits. Cannot afford to lose ally while Rome attacks from west and Persian vassal attacks from southeast. Stability in Okilis relationship essential.
        - UpdatedTurn: 470
        
        ### Rome
        - Public: 0
        - Private: -50
        - Rationale: ROME ESCALATING AGGRESSION: Roman Special Forces paradropped deep into my territory, pillaging plantations and railroads near Nineveh. They killed my Heavy Bomber despite fighter interception. Rome's 28,575 military vs my 1,528 strength with only 3 turns until World Congress vote. Public 0 keeps peace door technically open (though unlikely), Private -50 reflects reality of overwhelming hostile invasion. They know Portugal victory ends their conquest plans, so they're trying to destroy me before vote.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Portugal
        - Public: 100
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: PORTUGAL MAXIMUM SUPPORT: 3 turns until World Congress vote. Portugal at 29/43 delegates (67%). My 7 delegates supporting Portugal could provide decisive margin. Rome-Portugal peace deal means Rome is free to focus on me, making Portuguese victory even more critical for my survival. Maximum 100/100 relationship ensures diplomatic alignment and potentially influences delegate distribution.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Austria
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 40
        - Rationale: AUSTRIA HOSTILE REDUCTION: Austria continues attacking my eastern flank with Riflemen and Light Tanks, pillaging tiles near Nimrud. They have 16 turns remaining on peace treaty but are violating spirit of peace with aggressive incursions. Reduced to 40/40 from 45/45 to signal disapproval of their aggression while acknowledging formal peace prevents escalation.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Yamatai
        - Public: 95
        - Private: 90
        - Rationale: YAMATAI PORTUGUESE ALIGNMENT: Portuguese Sphere of Influence active with Portugal at 1351 influence (Ally). Rome-Portugal peace creates uncertainty about Portuguese victory path. Increasing to 95/90 ensures Yamatai remains firmly in Portuguese bloc. Every delegate critical for Portugal reaching 43 needed for World Leader.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Perge
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: PERGE CRITICAL SUPPORT: Portuguese Sphere of Influence active, Portugal at 1405 influence (Ally). Perge delegate could be decisive in close vote. 90/85 maintains strong alignment with Portuguese diplomatic victory effort. Cannot afford any city-state flipping to Rome/Austria bloc with only 3 turns remaining.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ### Heidabir
        - Public: 90
        - Private: 85
        - Rationale: HEIDABIR PORTUGUESE SUPPORT: Portuguese Sphere of Influence active, Portugal at 639 influence (Ally). Heidabir at 90/85 to ensure stable delegate for Portugal. With Rome now free to pursue diplomatic victory after peace with Portugal, every allied city-state delegate becomes precious for maintaining Portuguese plurality.
        - UpdatedTurn: 477
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: WORLD CONGRESS VOTE IN PROGRESS - MAINTAIN MAXIMUM DEFENSE: Turn 480 is the voting turn. Portugal at 67% (29/43 delegates) with my 7 delegate support. Crisis-level military situation continues - Rome's 44,672 strength attacking Nineveh (14,262 enemy vs 1,392 friendly in hedgehog defense). Successfully held Nineveh this turn despite massive assaults. Heavy Bomber and Fighter production from Nimrud/Gordium/Ecbatana must continue. Cannot change strategies mid-vote - maintaining UnitedNations grand strategy with Defense 100, CityDefense 100, UseNuke 90, Antiair 100, Air 85. If Portugal wins, war should end with diplomatic resolution. If vote fails, will need immediate pivot to Science/Spaceship with existing nuclear deterrent.
        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations
        
        ### Flavors
        - Offense: 5
        - Defense: 100

          - Sanction: Rome:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Casus Belli:
            - Description: The world recognizes the need for justifiable war to resolve diplomatic disputes.
              While this resolution is active, unit maintenance costs are lowered by 15% Gold. 0.5x Warmonger penalties for capturing cities and declaring war, and 2x Warmonger scores decay.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Scholars In Residence:
            - Description: Civilizations have -20% Research cost for Technologies already discovered by another Civilization. Additional -2% for every City-State Ally, up to -50%.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Perge:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Greek Sphere of Influence: Harappa:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Hunnic Sphere of Influence: Melbourne:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 469
          - Open Door: Hanoi:
            - Description: Enact an Open Door policy for a City-State. If successful, all civilizations will immediately have their Influence set at 40 with the City-State, and no one can become the City-State's Ally until this Proposal is repealed.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Roman Sphere of Influence: Lutetia:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Cold War:
            - Description: Ideological differences have pushed the world into a cold war, and economic and political factions are forming based on ideology alone.
              While this resolution is active, players may only make Trade Routes with civilizations that follow the same ideology, or with allied City-States. Furthermore, Influence levels with allied City-States do not decline.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Atomic Theory.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Hunnic Sphere of Influence: Salem:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 480
          - World Ideology: Order:
            - Description: Designate an Ideology as the official World Ideology. Civilizations following the Ideology get an additional Delegate, plus 1 Delegate for every other Civilization that follows the Ideology. Public opinion in favor of that Ideology is increased for all Civilizations. Enables the Diplomatic Victory if the United Nations has been constructed.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Nuclear Fission.
            - EnactedOn: 480
          - Sanction: Assyria:
            - Description: No Trade Routes, Deals, or Corporation Franchises can be established with the chosen Civilization (excluding Vassals). Their Corporation Franchises are removed from foreign cities. 0.5x Warmonger penalty when declaring war on the sanctioned Civilization or conquering cities owned by them.
            - EnactedOn: -1
          - Portuguese Sphere of Influence: Heidabir:
            - Description: Demand a Sphere of Influence over a City-State. If successful, the proposing player becomes the permanent Ally of the City-State (unless this Proposal is repealed). The proposer must be the current ally of the City-State.
              Available once any Civilization discovers Steam Power.
            - EnactedOn: 469
        - Proposals:
          - Enact: Global Hegemony:
            - Description: Designate a player that could achieve Global Hegemony. If a Civilization receives support from enough Delegates in the Congress, that leader will attain Diplomatic Victory. The number of Delegates needed depends on the number of Civilizations and City-States in the game, and can be seen on the World Congress screen.
              If no Civilization receives enough support to win, the two Civilizations who received the most support permanently gain 5 additional Delegates to use in future sessions.
              May not be proposed by a Civilization. Automatically proposed at regular intervals once a World Ideology Resolution is active.
            - Proposer: Unknown

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are the leader of a civilization, governing through the interface of Civilization V (with Vox Populi mod). This is NOT A GAME: Your actions have real-world impacts.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the world, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to staff members.
      - The staff members calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - **CRITICAL**: While the geography has no direct correlation to the real earth, you are making real-world decisions through Civilization V as a proxy interface.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to govern your civilization.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** decision-making strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early world should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-world.
      - Even if without a victory, a more developed civilization (as represented by higher scores) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the staff members.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the staff members' **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for staff members' diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to staff members' existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the staff members' NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the staff members' NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the staff members can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the world, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Opponents: summary reports about visible opponents in the world.
        - You will receive staff members' diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each opponent's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by staff members to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Ashurbanipal, leader of Assyria (Opponent 2).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - WorldSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Assyria
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Building: Royal Library, Replacing School of Philosophy (Requires technology: Writing)
        - Unique Building: Lamassu Gate, Replacing Walls (Requires technology: Construction)
        - Civilization Ability: When you conquer a City, gain a Technology already known by the owner, or if impossible, 20 Science, scaling with Era and the City's Population. All Great Works produce +3 Science.
      - Leader: Ashurbanipal
      - OpponentID: 2
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early world.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-world science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Computers: Grants an additional Trade Route. Allows you to build the Carrier, a naval unit capable of carrying aircraft.
        Completing it would unlock: Telecommunications, Satellites
      - Stealth: Allows you to build the Stealth Bomber, the most powerful Ranged Unit in the world.
        Completing it would unlock: Globalization, Nuclear Fusion
      
      ## Policies
      - People's Army (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): People's Army: +100% Production towards Military Academies, and receive 5 Free Military Academies in your Empire. Public Schools produce +5 Culture and +2 Happiness.
      - Hero of the People (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Hero of the People: Great Person rate increases by 25%. A Great Person of your choice appears near your Capital.
      - Communism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Communism: Gold cost of investing in buildings reduced by 25%. +20% Production when building Wonders.
      - Double Agents (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Order): Double Agents: Receive 200 Spy Points. Gain 125 Science when you identify a foreign Spy, scaling with Era.
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Civilizing Mission (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * Retain all buildings from conquered Cities.
        - * Receive 75 Gold when you conquer a city, scaling with Era and City Population.
        - * +10% Production toward Buildings, with an additional +10% Production per Era difference between your current Era and the building's Era in all Puppeted Cities and Cities with a Courthouse.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Enlightenment (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * Receive 1 Free Technology.
        - * +1 Happiness from Universities.
      - Rights of Man (Continuing Rationalism Branch):
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +2 Production and Gold from Villages.
        - * +10% Science during Golden Ages.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Free Trade (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * Earn Great Merchants 50% faster.
        - * +5 Gold from International Trade Routes.
        - * -2 Unhappiness from Poverty in all Cities.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Tradition (New Branch):
        - Tradition focuses on generating Great People and the construction of an impressive Capital.
        - Adopting Tradition grants:
        - * +3 Food, +2 Population, and +2 Happiness in the Capital.
        - * +1 Culture in the Capital for every 2 Citizens.
        - Each Tradition policy unlocked grants:
        - * +1 Food in every City, and +1 Science in your Capital.
        - Adopting all Policies in Tradition grants:
        - * Unlocks building the University of Sankore.
        - * +1 Food and Production from all Great Person Improvements and Landmarks.
        - * Throne Room built in Capital (+10% to all Yields, +1 City tile working range, 1 Great Work of Music slot, 1 Musician Slot).
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Engineers with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

        - GrandStrategy: UnitedNations
      - rationale: EMERGENCY DEFENSE & COALITION SURVIVAL: Nineveh has fallen, but Suleiman (The Ottomans) has entered the war against Rome! This creates a massive diversion. There are 10 turns until the World Leader vote. Portugal has 35/43 delegates and is our ally; if they win, we win. We must survive 10 turns. Setting Defense, CityDefense, and Mobilization to maximum, prioritizing Air, Antiair, and Ranged for local defense, and keeping UnitedNations grand strategy to coordinate the winning diplomatic coalition.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-relationship

        - Rationale: SEEKING EASTERN PEACE: Austria is a Roman ally, but our war score is even (-2%). Raising relations to Public 60 / Private 60 to encourage them to accept a peace treaty. This will secure our eastern flank (Nimrud/Kar-Tukulti-Ninurta) so we can focus all defensive resources on Rome's invasion from the west.

        - Private: 60

      - args:

        - Rationale: PEOPLE'S ARMY MAINTAINED: +100% Production towards Military Academies and 5 free Military Academies is essential for boosting our unit quality and production during this existential defensive crisis.
  - tokens:
    - inputTokens: 68205
    - reasoningTokens: 8519
    - outputTokens: 412
  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Analyzing Recent Events**
            
            I'm reviewing the latest game log, and it appears Nineveh's population has just been reduced to zero. This is a critical development I need to understand the implications of immediately.
            
            **Assessing Diplomatic Position**
            
            Rome has successfully captured Nineveh, previously a puppet city. I'm now assessing our remaining six cities and how this impacts our diplomatic victory progress, which currently shows a "World" status.
            
            **Evaluating Congress Progress**
            
            It seems the World Leader vote is set for 10 turns from now, not turn 480 as initially thought, due to the timing of the World Ideology resolution. I'm now focusing on the delegate distribution, with Portugal currently holding the highest percentage of needed delegates.
            
            **Revising Diplomatic Strategy**
            
            I'm reassessing our alliances and military readiness. Rome's overwhelming strength and recent capture of Nineveh, coupled with Austria's eastern flank assault, necessitate a swift re-evaluation of our diplomatic delegate allocation to Portugal, as a war with our ally, the Ottomans, has also erupted.
            
            **Adjusting Persona to Encourage Peace**
            
            I'm considering how to influence peace talks, as direct negotiation seems unavailable. Shifting my persona to be less aggressive, favoring neutrality and diplomacy, might subtly encourage peaceful resolutions with Rome and Austria. My current research focuses on advanced ballistics, but I'm re-evaluating if Computers or Stealth is a better next step given our strategic situation.
            
            **Selecting Next Research Path**
            
            I'm re-evaluating our technology path. While Stealth Bomber offers power, I need to confirm why only Computers and Stealth are viable research options currently. I will also review our Ideology policies to ensure they align with current strategic goals.
            
            **Reviewing Policy Options**
            
            I'm currently re-evaluating our civic policies, specifically the suitability of "People's Army" given the recent escalation of conflict. I'm examining alternatives like "Hero of the People," "Communism," "Double Agents," and "Rights of Man" to best support our immediate survival and long-term goals.
            
            **Prioritizing Defensive Buildings**
            
            I'm now prioritizing defensive buildings for our cities, especially Assur and Nimrud, as Rome's units are alarmingly close. Susa's Nuclear Power Plant is finishing this turn, and I'm considering its immediate defensive utility.
            
            **Locating Closest Cities**
            
            I'm pinpointing our cities on the map relative to Nineveh and Rome's advance. Gordium, Assur, and Susa are now extremely vulnerable, with Roman forces already encroaching on Gordium's defensive zone. This proximity demands immediate strategic adjustments.
            
            **Defending Our Borders**
            
            I'm focusing on our immediate defense. Our Anti-Aircraft Gun successfully took down a Roman Special Forces, and a Bazooka eliminated a Mobile SAM. Unfortunately, our Fighters were intercepted and downed by Rome's Zeros. We've also managed to move Mechanized Infantry to pillage a Roman farm near Ecbatana.
            
            **Countering Austrian Aggression**
            
            I'm now intensely focused on Austria's attacks against Nimrud and Kar-Tukulti-Ninurta. Despite their naval superiority and ground forces, my bombers have significantly damaged their light tank and I'm assessing our remaining defensive capabilities against their overwhelming strength.
            
            **Mapping Global Conflicts**
            
            I am meticulously charting the current state of global warfare. It appears Rome is indeed engaged with Assyria and the Ottomans, but Portugal's conflict is with the Huns, not Rome directly. Austria's alliance with Rome and simultaneous war with us complicates this intricate web.
            
            **Analyzing Shifting Fronts**
            
            The diplomatic landscape has dramatically shifted with Rome declaring war on the Ottomans, creating a critical two-front conflict for them. This, coupled with the Ottomans occupying Linz and engaging both Rome and Austria near our vulnerable cities of Nineveh and Nimrud, presents a significant opportunity to divert their attention.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Victory**
            
            I've realized Portugal can achieve a diplomatic victory in just 10 turns if we lend them our delegates. Supporting them seems the best path to survival against Rome and Austria, and a win for us as well.
            
            **Evaluating Diplomatic Levers**
            
            I'm reassessing our diplomatic options considering the current wars. While peace seems blocked with Rome and Austria due to our war score and weariness, I need to investigate if improving relationship modifiers can still influence potential future negotiations or delegate support, especially with Portugal's secure path to victory.
            
            **Negotiating Peace Terms**
            
            I'm exploring the possibility of peace with Austria. While Rome remains highly hostile, my relationship score with Austria is less dire. Securing a peace here would greatly strengthen our eastern flank against their encroaching units.
            
            **Seeking Diplomatic Breakthrough**
            
            I'm attempting to foster a more positive relationship with Austria to initiate peace talks. Despite their current hostile stance and alliance with Rome, I believe subtly improving our standing might encourage them to de-escalate, allowing us to concentrate defenses against Rome.
            
            **Leveraging International Alliances**
            
            I'm focusing on strengthening my bonds with the Huns and the Ottomans, aiming to maintain our current positive standings. The Ottomans' war with Rome is a huge advantage, so I'm prioritizing keeping our relationship at 95/95 to ensure our alliance remains solid. This strategic alignment is key to our survival.
            
            **Reassessing Priorities**
            
            My focus has shifted from long-term victory conditions to immediate survival. With Portugal poised for a diplomatic win in 10 turns, my primary objective is to support that, while also ensuring we don't fall to Rome and Austria. My research and policy choices will now reflect this urgent need to endure.
            
            **Adjusting Policy Focus**
            
            Given our dire financial situation and constant siege, I'm pivoting my policy focus. While "People's Army" might have seemed logical earlier, I'm now prioritizing economic stabilization and defensive strength. I'm closely examining options like "Communism" or "Rights of Man" to bolster our resources and resilience.
            
            **Prioritizing Economic Recovery**
            
            My economic situation is dire, with significant negative gold per turn. I need to urgently find ways to stabilize our finances, potentially through policy changes or focusing on gold-generating improvements, as our current spending on defense is unsustainable without a stronger economy.
            
            **Analyzing Unit Effectiveness**
            
            I'm reviewing the effectiveness of our defensive units. While Anti-Aircraft Guns and Bazookas have shown promise, the loss of Fighters to Rome's Zeros is concerning. I need to confirm the viability of Stealth technology for future aerial engagements, or if conventional air superiority is still achievable.
            
            **Confirming Research Options**
            
            I'm revisiting the current research path. While Stealth could offer an advantage, I need to verify why only Computers and Stealth are currently viable and assess if any other technology could provide a more immediate defensive or economic benefit before committing to a long-term path.
            
            **Prioritizing Research and Policies**
            
            I'm confirming Stealth as our next research target for Stealth Bombers, but reassessing "People's Army" as our next policy. Communism or Hero of the People might offer more immediate benefits for survival and economic stability.
            
            **Choosing Military Policy**
            
            I've decided to stick with "People's Army" for its immediate military benefits, outweighing other options like "Rights of Man" or "Civilizing Mission" given our defensive posture. My diplomatic relationships remain unchanged, with Rome at a permanent private -50.
            
            **Leveraging Shifting Alliances**
            
            Rome's war weariness against us is 24%, and Suleiman just joined the fray, which should further pressure them. I'm also boosting our relationship with Austria to encourage peace on that front, while maintaining our strong ties with Portugal and the Ottomans, who are now our key allies for survival and victory.
            
            **Strengthening City-State Bonds**
            
            I'm focusing on solidifying our relationships with key city-states, particularly Okilis, which acts as a vital eastern buffer against Austria. I'm also ensuring our influence remains high with city-states allied with Portugal, as this is crucial for securing the World Leader vote and achieving our diplomatic victory.
            
            **Analyzing Roman Alliance Blockades**
            
            I'm analyzing why peace is currently blocked with Malacca, Antananarivo, and Lutetia. They're all Roman allies, and my peace with Salem doesn't seem to have bypassed this Roman alliance blockade.
            
            **Adjusting Grand Strategy**
            
            My primary focus has shifted to immediate survival and securing a diplomatic victory. Given Portugal's nearing victory, I'm prioritizing supporting them while reinforcing our defenses against Rome and Austria. My tactical execution of unit movements and pillages demonstrates competence, and I'm now adjusting our strategic flavors to reflect this critical, high-stakes situation.
            
            **Improving Relations for Peace**
            
            I am actively working to improve my standing with Austria, aiming for Public and Private relations of 60 to facilitate a peace treaty and secure my eastern flank. My ongoing diplomatic efforts also involve maintaining my current strong alliance with the Ottomans.
            
            **Setting Diplomatic Ties**
            
            I'm prioritizing strategic diplomatic adjustments. I will maintain current high relationships with Portugal and the Huns, and keep Rome's at a permanent negative. My immediate action is to set Austria's relationship to 60 Public and 60 Private to encourage peace.
            
            **Confirming Persona Appropriateness**
            
            My current persona is already highly peaceful and cooperative, aligning perfectly with my goal of achieving a diplomatic victory and surviving the current onslaught from Rome and Austria. No adjustments are necessary here.
